# HTP MoE decode benchmark — LFM2.5-8B-A1B on the Galaxy S25 Ultra

Contract: `docs/plans/0001-htp-moe-decode-agent-system.md`. The supervisor
edits this file from filled `docs/measurements/*.md`; nobody else writes
numbers here.

## Method

`nntrainer_causallm` on the phone, `NNTR_NUM_THREADS=8`, prompt 512 tokens,
generation 64 / 512 / 1024 tokens, two runs per cell, non-profile binary.
Decode tok/s is reported over the whole generation and over the last 64
tokens. Every sitting starts with variant A (the unchanged reference
binary) and every other variant is read against it inside that sitting.
Accuracy: generated text identical to the CPU `q40` run of the same
prompt (y/n), `NNTR_L2_DIFF` when DSP arithmetic changed. For variants
that run the per-token entry (#130 onward) the amended contract §1 gate
applies: decode PPL (`NNTR_PPL_DECODE`, PR #144) against variant A of
the same sitting (≤ +2 %), the text column vs A, and the user's
`text approved: y/n`; text identity cannot hold for a resident kind on
this quantized pipeline (LEDGER rule 39), so a differing text is folded
only when approved.

Unit: **any Galaxy S25 Ultra** (user decision 2026-09-22, contract §12).
A handoff never names a serial; the filled handoff records the serial
actually used, every row here carries it, and numbers are compared only
as an A/B inside one sitting. Where two sittings happen to share a unit,
the same-unit drift is recorded as a note (LEDGER rule 13). Rows from the
PR author's device are labelled `(PR)`. Rows #77, #94, #88, #105, #100 and #113 ran on `R3CY205ZMND` (SM-S938N);
**#117 ran on `R3CY10WM83Y`** (also SM-S938N, the user's second unit).
Its untouched DMA anchor cell reads **37.3 GB/s** where `R3CY205ZMND`
reads 31.2 (LEDGER rule 34), so a `mm` number is never compared across
the two units unscaled. Until cycle 16 the NPU **"now"** was **#117's variant A of its
second (cooled) sitting** (2026-09-23, `6412bfc9`, `R3CY10WM83Y`) =
`htp_moe` head code (`763762a2`) with the **D192 default** of PR #115
(`c45d4433`): one-row loop + 192 KB `l2fetch` lead, banner
`[HTP] moe m1 gemv: on (applied=0x103c1) lead=192KB rows1=1 feed=default source=env`
(`NNTR_MOE_HTP_M1_GEMV=1` was explicit in every #117 cell, hence
`source=env`; an unset run prints `source=default`). PR #118's branch
(`3c4912fa`) adds only the opt-in feed path that A does not run. This is
the first sitting whose A is D192, so it replaces #100 A (27.85 / 27.09 /
26.45, `R3CY205ZMND`) as rule 33 required; A0 = `NNTR_MOE_HTP_GEMV_LEAD_KB=0
NNTR_MOE_HTP_GEMV_ROWS1=0` (`applied=0xc1`, the pre-#115 default) read
27.56 / 26.78 / 26.20 in the same sitting, so **D192 is +2.41 / +4.16 /
+3.91 %** there (sitting 1: +3.56 / +2.41 / +3.93) — #113's +4.11 / +3.29 /
+4.26 % confirmed on a second unit. Cross-unit, this A is +1.3 / +3.0 /
+3.0 % over #100 A, inside the 6–8.6 % unit band (rule 10): not a reading.
**Next default, in progress (PR #118, #117): the VTCM DMA feed**
(`HVX_GEMV_M1_FEED` → `1u`, banner `feed=vtcm`), measured as #117's B at
**37.38 / 36.49 / 35.01** (+32.5 / +30.9 / +28.6 % vs A, text identical in
36/36 runs over two sittings, M==1 `mm` 931.9 → 676.4 µs). Until it is
measured as the control of its own sitting it is a **lever cell**, not
the "now" (contract §4.2). From the flip on, variant A of every sitting
is the feed default and its log must print exactly one banner with
`feed=vtcm source=default` (the `applied=` word is fixed by the flip
commit and recorded in handoff 117's "Landed defaults" section); an A
log with `feed=default` or `feed=arena` is the pre-flip default and voids
the sitting (rule 21). The old cell stays reachable as A0 =
`NNTR_MOE_HTP_GEMV_FEED=0` (= this sitting's A); the pre-#115 cell as
`LEAD_KB=0 ROWS1=0 FEED=0`.
**Since cycle 16 the NPU "now" is #120's variant A** (`120-upstream-4327-sync.md`
@ `7ded3ce9`, `R3CY10WM83Y`, 2026-09-23 17:55–18:08): `htp_moe` @ `d79c0efe`
(PR #118 merged) run with nothing set, banner
`[HTP] moe m1 gemv: on (applied=0x303e1) lead=192KB rows1=1 feed=vtcm source=default`
— the first sitting whose control is the feed default, as the previous
paragraph required. It reads **35.97 / 35.96 / 35.17** at G 64 / 512 / 1024
(#117 B, the same code as a lever cell, read 37.38 / 36.49 / 35.01 on the
same unit: −3.8 / −1.5 / +0.5 %, inside rule 9's same-unit drift). Its A0
(`NNTR_MOE_HTP_GEMV_FEED=0`, `applied=0x103e1 … feed=arena`) read 28.62 at
G=64, so the feed is **+25.7 %** as a control (+32.5 % as #117's lever); an A
log without `feed=vtcm source=default` is the pre-#118 default and voids the
sitting. The same sitting's B is the upstream PR #4327 sync (PR #121):
decode +4.7 / +0.9 / −0.2 %, prefill +12.4 / +10.2 / +6.2 %, text identical.
PR #121 merged as `b7d46b57` (2026-09-27), so from the next sitting on the
variant A code carries the sync; its A is read against #120 A as a control
(expect prefill up ≈ 10 %, decode unmoved), and the first A row on a
post-#121 skel records that skel's md5 (none recorded at merge, LEDGER §3a).
**PR #108 merged as `8e121dbe` (cycle 10): every variant A is the GEMV
default** (then `[HTP] moe m1 gemv: on (applied=0x1) source=default`,
superseded by the D192 banner above since PR #115; an A log without
`source=default` voids the sitting) — #100's six A logs all carry it
exactly once. #108 had no sitting of its own; its ride-along cells rode
#100's handoff and are read there (A vs A0 = **+13.5 %** at G=64, the
fixed M==1 row, the GEMV-vs-HMX transport line). The CPU "now" stays
from #94 sitting 2 (`dad0f476`); none of #88, #105, #100 or #113 ran a
CPU cell.
**Cycle 19 (2026-09-28): #130's sitting is the first device row of the
post-#121 tree** (`130-per-token-wiring.md` @ `d3a02a66` on
`htp/130-per-token-wiring`, `R3CY10WM83Y`, 13:32–14:10 KST, code
`htp_moe` @ `9deea6e2`). **Its A does not replace the "now"** (rules 9,
13, 20, 22): (1) the phone was warm from a voided first pass (battery
33.1 → 41.2 °C, no cool-down), A's own r1/r2 spread reached 18 % at G=64
(36.12 vs 29.81) and every A cell sits −8.4 / −8.5 / −10.9 % under #120 A
on the same unit while the sync itself read decode unmoved and prefill
+10 % as #120's B — a thermal reading, not a code one; (2) every cell ran
on a **local rebuild with one uncommitted define** (`-DENABLE_HEXKL=1`
on `libcausallm_core.so`, #135) — the committed app carried no live
`NNTR_HTP_FORWARD` path (LEDGER rule 36), so no binary of that sitting is

`Android.mk` exports the define; the next sitting's A is the first
committed-tree A with the hooks compiled in). The sitting's A/B/C verdict
stands (one binary set switched by env, rule 21): B (all six kinds
resident, 95 calls/token) **−22 / −25 / −30 %** with a **degenerate text
(user: `text approved: n`)**, C (`KINDS=MOE`, 22 calls) within A's drift
with text ≡ A (`y`). The "now" stays #120 A until a cooled sitting on a
committed app reads the post-#121 default as its own control. From this
sitting on the `decode tok/s (last 64)` column is filled (PR #128).
**Cycle 21 (2026-09-29): the dspqueue MoE transport is the default and
the NPU "now" is #141's variant Q** (`141-dspq-moe.md`, on `htp_moe` since
PR #151; `R3CY10WM83Y`, 2026-09-28 21:2x–21:5x KST, cool start 26.5 °C;
set built from the committed tree `07fb1938`, device md5 = staged). Q =
the 22 M==1 MoE calls through dspqueue (`NNTR_HTP_DSPQ=1`, DSP spin 1 ms),
read against A (FastRPC, the then-default) in one mirrored sitting: **decode
36.78 → 39.40 (+7.1 %) at G=64, 35.98 → 37.77 (+5.0 %) at G=512**,
transport 87.9 → 14.1 µs/call, MoE dumps `bit_identical=1` against A (2862
files, A2 null check = 1), text ≡ A on the 8-prompt set and in every tok/s
cell. PR #151 (`08de92b1`) merged it with the flip commit `c73384d2` (unset =
on, `NNTR_HTP_DSPQ=0` = FastRPC; device check: unset prints `dspq: on`
once, `=0` none, texts identical). Why Q moves the "now" without waiting for
a control sitting, unlike #117 B → #120 A: (1) Q is bit-identical to A at
every MoE call, so a control sitting could only re-read the transport,
which this sitting already read as a same-sitting A/B; (2) the flip commit
changes nothing but the unset value of the env parse, and its banner was
checked on the device; (3) the sitting is cool, committed and mirrored,
which #130's A was not. **G=1024 was not measured**: that cell stays
#120 A's 35.17 (FastRPC, D192 + feed), marked as not re-read, until the
next sitting's A fills it. **From the next sitting on, variant A must print
`[HTP] dspq: on …` exactly once and `dspq: close calls=N served=N bad=0` at
exit (N = 22 × decode steps); an A log with no `dspq: on` line or with
`dspq: off (…)` is the FastRPC path and voids the sitting (rule 36). A0 for
the transport is `NNTR_HTP_DSPQ=0`.** The same sitting's A (36.78 / 35.98)
and #134's A (36.90 / 36.45, 20:11 KST, `bb845426`) are the first cool,
committed post-#121 FastRPC controls; they agree with #120 A within
+2.3 / +0.1 % and +2.6 / +1.4 %, so the upstream sync and PRs #143–#145
left the switch-off decode where it was.
**Cycle 22 (2026-09-29): the DSP L2 bypass on the MoE weight DMA is the
default and the NPU "now" is #158's variant B** (`158-dma-bypass.md`, on

every arena-backed expert weight descriptor, M==1 feed and M>1 HMX alike),

**decode 38.61 → 51.82 (+34.2 %) at G=64, 37.40 → 50.61 (+35.3 %) at

`bit_identical=1` (2862 files, A2 null = 1), decode nll lines B == A byte
for byte, text ≡ A on 8/8 prompts and 6/6 tok/s cells. The flip commit
`21169f6e` (in PR #159) changes only the unset value: unset = bypass
(`applied=0x703e1 … dma_bypass=1`), `NNTR_MOE_DMA_BYPASS=0` = the L2 path
(`0x303e1`). B moves the "now" without a control sitting for #141 Q's three
reasons (bit-identical, flip = unset value only, cool committed mirrored
sitting), **with one difference: the device banner check of the flip is
still pending** (the phone was disconnected when `21169f6e` was written;
host checks and the in-process E2E pass). **The next sitting's A must print
`applied=0x703e1 … dma_bypass=1 source=default` once; an A log that prints
`dma_bypass=0` without `NNTR_MOE_DMA_BYPASS=0` set is the L2 path and voids
the sitting (rule 36). A0 for the bypass is `NNTR_MOE_DMA_BYPASS=0`.** The
same sitting's A (38.61 / 37.40 / 36.17) and #90's A (39.70 / 39.18 /
**36.53**, 11:07–11:10, `NNTR_OP_TIME=1`, one run per G) are the first
G=1024 cells on the dspqueue default; #120 A's (35.17) placeholder is
retired. Prefill: H5 read on the mirrored means is −8.3 % at G=512 (A
461.95 vs B 423.45) because A's run 1 (501.5) was the sitting's coolest
cell; the adjacent cells B r1 / B r2 / A r2 read 425.2 / 421.7 / 422.4
(±1 %), G 64 / 1024 read +0.4 / +0.6 %, and the level-2 M>1 `dsp` is
15127 → 14724 µs/call (−2.7 %). Read as prefill equal within temperature
(the #117 tie-breaker), not as a regression; the cool-start A of the next
sitting re-reads it.

**#170 S1 / S2** (`170-attn-m1-hf.md`, merged with PR #176 `90d88e2b`; S1

lm_head VTCM-fed 7.88 ms/token vs the CPU's 7.4, heap 113 MiB free vs 383
needed → decision D (user). **#178** (`178-second-dsp-session.md` on

`htp/170-round2`, PR #182 open; 32.8 °C start): round 2 bit-identical

7 %; default off. **The "now" (51.82 / 50.61 / 47.36, #158 B) is not

map window is **not viable** — fastrpc map + unmap of an 11 MiB window

for 22 layers, re-attach by copy 23.8 ms/token, a fresh mapping costs
the first FC call +115 µs, `HAP_mmap` is not available to the unsigned PD
(`0x80000402`), lm_head 140 MiB whole map 2.84 ms or 12 slices ≈ 15
ms/token; stop rule (> 1.0 ms/token) hit, sanities before / after pass, no

**Cycle 27 (2026-10-01, fold of #201's three sittings and #207's farm
sitting; base `htp_decode`): no new default, the "now" stays the record

Decode step 1 of p01 is a near-tie (A's `town` p = 0.134, nll 2.010; B / D pick `final` with `town` at nll 2.103 / 2.113): the teacher-forced PPL cannot see the free-running continuation after a flip, which is what the approval caught (LEDGER §2 #134 row) and what the direction change answered.** **#130 (cycle 19): C (`KINDS=MOE`) ≡ A 6/6, approved `y`; B (all six kinds resident) differs at the first word in 6/6 cells and loops ("The final answer should be the same as the original, but you must not stop until you are told to."), approved `n` → fail, filed as #136; the PPL column was n/a by construction (`NNTR_PPL` scores prefill only, #134). Ride-along gtests on the same skel: `HvxM1Ops` 0/5, `HvxAttnM1` 2/4 — bit-exact on normal-range rows, a few ulp off on the subnormal / tiny-value rows, `RejectsBadShapes` answers `AEE_ERPC` (LEDGER rules 37–38, #137)**. **#136 (cycle 20 close, `R3CY10WM83Y`, G=64 ×1 + G=4 dumps): not a defect — every kind alone and every pair ≡ switch-off except RMSNORM + ROPE + ATTN_M1; switch-off and `KINDS=MOE` bit-identical run to run; one resident kind moves the first decode MoE input to 34–40 dB, two stacked flip the token at pos 512; each dumped RMSNORM recomputed in NEON order is 131–148 dB from the DSP (DSP nearer f64). Rule 39: text identity cannot hold for any resident kind; the gate is decode PPL vs A + text + approval (#134 / PR #144). The pool race fixed by PR #143 is real but was not the cause** |

#132 Part B side tables (unit `R3CY10WM83Y`, 2026-09-30 06:13–09:2x KST,
five sets on `htp/132-partb-e3` (`10bb91b7` → `54a30c7f` → `a0bf121b` /
`376279fc`) with the shadow branch `dev/e2e-shadow-132`; the handoff
`132-part-b-e2e.md` on the branch carries E5–E5c as read, E5e / E5d as
staged, and issue #132's comments carry E5e / E5d as read; verdict
LEDGER §2 #132 Part B row, rules 54–56):

* **E5 (06:13–06:39):** every E / Ev died at load: S2 opened (`s2_session=1 s2_effdom=7 open_ms=21.2 open_path=1 hmx=0 vtcm_kib=0`) and placed its FC set (`attach_mib=383.6 chunks=2 mapped_mib=448 load_ms=788.6`), then **S1's arena failed at `fastrpc_mmap(64 MiB) … mapped=3584 MiB in 14 chunks`** (3696 needed). The rule: whichever session maps while the other is open and mapped gets 14 × 256 MiB; S1 mapped first reaches 3840 and S2 then maps 3584 beside it (#178 Q1). Fix `ba486d16` / `54a30c7f`: S2 opens after `repack_weight` has mapped S1's arena (banner `s1_arena_mib`, expected 3840). S (95 calls, every shadow, G=8): every record equal.
* **E5b (06:55–07:32):** loads and runs — S2 `open_ms` 24.0, `attach_mib` 383.6, `load_ms` 684.9, startup 15 049 vs A 14 706 ms; **`calls/token=1.00`, `hops/token=44.00`**, timeouts / stale / `id_mismatch` 0; MoE prefill dumps E == A (46 files `bit_identical=1`); G=8 shadows equal, Ev logits == S0, nll S0 / S / Ev == A. **Bit identity fails past step 5–26**: G=256 forced on A's ids, E's nll differs on 8/8 prompts (first step p01 25, p04 5, p07 26; top1 255/256, 245/256), text ≡ A on p02 p03 p05 p06 p08, the G=512 free run differs; the host build (x86) does not reproduce it (E3 == E1 == D over 256 steps). **Speed E 16.7 / 16.0 / 15.2** vs A 53.5–55.1 / 54.4–54.8 / 52.4–52.9: S1 46.7 M pcyc / token wait 24.3 ms, S2 61.7 M wait 23.2, first token 55.4 ms; the sessions strictly alternate, 108.4 M pcyc / 59 ms ≈ 1.8 GHz (not the clock); S1's 22 MoE + router rounds ≈ 2.7× the isolated MoE's cycles. Suspect from the code: `tk_take` spins 1000 µs without a pause and with a flush-invalidate per iteration; the pools' post-job spin (≈ 100 µs) does the same at each hop. The runner's attention-cache pattern expected #170's 24 576 KiB, the branch prints 49 152 (pre-#170 kernel).
* **E5c (08:05–08:12, per prompt: A, S0 = the CPU's MoE rows, S with every shadow, Ev with S2's hop rows, G=64 forced):** **S and Ev leave A at the same step** — prompt512 step 25 (`E2E TRACE first_diff=step24/pos536/L13.in`), korean step 5 (`step4/pos330/L17.in`) — so the two-session mechanism is not the cause; every shadow equal over 64 steps (fc 4744/4744, add 3072/3072, router 1408/1408, swiglu 128/128, argmax 65/65, norm 3136 + 15 360 heads, attn 12 288/12 288). L13 / L17 are conv layers; **CONV1D_GATE was the one resident op without a shadow**: its spec `m1_conv_gate_det` was written in the HVX kernel's order `(w0·g + w1·s1) + w2·s0` (every op rounded) and never held against the CPU, whose decode (`neon::causal_depthwise_conv1d_k3_decode`) is `vmulq(w0, x)` + two `vfmaq` = `fma(w2, s0, fma(w1, s1, w0·g))`; on the host check's inputs the two differ by 1 ulp on 2075 / 2694 of 16 384 outputs, and out_proj's Q8 quantizer hides almost all of it. Fix `a0bf121b`: spec and `hvx_conv_gate_m1_f32` in the CPU's fused order (vector `a·c` and `w0·g`, the two taps as scalar `sffma`, then `b·y`); `m1_ops_host_check` also holds the kernel against an independent `fmaf` model. Open: the prefill kernel `hvx_conv_gate_f32` is unfused too (only the HTP conv-block prefill engine uses it, not the decode path).
* **E5e (08:40–, conv fix on silicon, sh adds the conv shadow tag 8):** **S and Ev bit-identical over 64 steps on prompt512 and bitset-04-korean** — logits / nll `first_diff=-`, `E2E TRACE first_diff=-`, every shadow equal incl. **conv 1152/1152**.
* **E5d (spin fix `376279fc` / `a7917394`: a paused spin of `NNTR_HTP_E2E_SPIN_US`, then 20 µs sleeps, pools parked before every post; the token response carries each side's wall, pcycles and per-kind op cycles):** E at spin 0 / 20 / 1000 µs, G=64, each with an A sanity and a text check → **spin 0 best: E 20.8 tok/s at G=64 (was 16.7), 19.5 at G=512**, text ≡ A. Per kind (G=64, ms/token): **S1 MoE 9.67 (0.44 per round = the isolated rate) + router 0.79; S2 FC 8.75, DENSE_FFN 5.60, LM_HEAD 5.97, ATTN_M1 8.33** (the pre-#170 kernel; the branch predates rounds 2–3), small ops ≈ 1.2; ARM token 44.7 ms. Next on the branch: rebase onto the re-rebased #175 / `htp_moe` (picks up ATTN_M1 round 3, ≈ −7 ms), the HVX quantizer / SwiGLU / argmax in the graph kernels and per-shape lanes (FC / FFN / lm_head 20.3 → ≈ 11 ms), then set_e5f; projected ≈ 25 ms/token ≈ 40 tok/s before the FC-loop work (plan's 47–51 needs the FC loop toward 57 GB/s).

Larger than S5-0's ≈ 1.6 ms estimate: the ARM remainder of contract §2 on this graph.
* **Reference gate (A16 vs F16, G 64, `NNTR_HTP_DUMP` + `NNTR_PPL_DECODE` runs):** `E2E eval gemma26-prefill-f-vs-off files=62 bit_identical=1 min_snr_db=inf`; tokens 60 / 65 — steps 1, 16, 33, 40, 57 (A 126049, F 82982; F's first 8 / 16 do not match: step 1 differs); A's margins at those steps 0.04095 / 0.00026 / 0.00762 / 0.01438 / 0.01225 vs 2·rms(F − A) 2.568 → 0 unexpected; the largest A margin in the run 0.156; null check A-on-A `top1=64/64 nll_sum=177.49488952694784` == S5-0; **F on A's ids `top1=59/64 nll_sum=185.06547` (ppl 18.023 vs 16.012)**; at every differing step F prefers 82982 by 1.08–1.37 logits where A had the two tied.
* **Load (F16 G64):** `[HTP] fc wh: file=…_fcwh.bin handles=0` (the banner before the bind), `graph: q4m1 weights=206 handles=16 feed=vtcm wh_handles=435`, `e2e: fc arena weights=206 handles=16 attach_mib=396.0 chunks=2 mapped_mib=448 feed=vtcm load_ms=2426.8 lanes=6,3 s1_arena_mib=704 s1_heap_kib=81619`, two `attn_m1` caches (25 × hd256 400 MiB + 5 × hd512 40 MiB), `arena: chunks unmapped 7/7 mib=1536`, close `heap_used_kib=603370`, `calls/token=1.00`; S1 ceiling 3840 after all 13 runs; `/data` 191 GB free; the device keeps `models/gemma4_26b/config.json` = the original (`config.4096.json` / `config.orig.json` beside it) and `models/gemma4_26b_fcwh/` (sidecar + symlinks) for the next sitting.
* **Page cache:** run `pgpgin` F 7.7–7.8 GB vs E 7.0–7.1 (the sidecar's 0.8 GB on top of the 7.2 GB bin, neither stays cached); decode-window `pgpgin` F 257–351 MiB vs E 336–439.

(`2a75f7d9`) added `moe_set_opts` and a 30th MoE stage slot, so the
reference set for sitting 2 was rebuilt from **`htp_moe` @ `2a75f7d9`**
(handoff `htp/94-sitting2-anchor-trace` @ `8029b76e`, worktree
`/home/j2z0-lee/nntrainer-94`, staged at `/local/mnt/workspace/htp_moe/94/`,
`md5.txt` there = the row below). Variant A of that sitting is this binary
with `NNTR_MOE_HTP_M1_GEMV` unset; variant C is the same binary with it set
(LEDGER ⑯). The two model `.bin`s are unchanged and keep the md5s above.
Pushing an old app with the new skel, or the reverse, fails with
`AEE_EBADPARM` (LEDGER rule 3).
